002     Shenzhen Investment Limited 深圳控股有限公司
        Annual Report 年報 2022




 Corporate Profile
 公司簡介




 公司深 耕 深 圳
   聚 焦大灣區
                                   The Company intensifies its development in
                                   Shenzhen and focuses on the Greater Bay Area

 Shenzhen Investment Limited has been listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited since 1997. Shum Yip
 Group, being its controlling shareholder, is an integrated conglomerate directly managed by State-owned Assets Supervision and Administration
 Commission of Shenzhen Municipal. Positioned as an “Innovative Constructor of Industrial Cities, Wealthy Livelihood Operator”, the Company’s core
 businesses include property development, property investment and comprehensive urban operation services, and it also engages in innovative businesses
 such as high-tech agriculture and high-end manufacturing, endeavoring to transform and upgrade into a technology-based industry group focusing on the
 development of urban complex and investment in technology industry.

 By intensifying its development in Shenzhen, focusing on the Guangdong-Hong Kong-Macao Greater Bay Area and planning for its development in
 the Yangtze River Delta and high-tier cities such as key provincial capital cities, the Group currently has a land reserve with gross floor area of
 approximately 6.60 million square meters in various cities of China, of which the quality land reserve with gross floor area of approximately
 4.28 million square meters is located in the Greater Bay Area. The Company is committed to optimizing its land reserves structure, and
 concentrates its assets and business in the Greater Bay Area as well as high-tier cities in key regions.

 It has all along been the Company’s core strategic goal to promote the long term sustainable development of its business, with
 an aim to bring a stable return and create value appreciation for its shareholders.

 深圳控股有限公司於1997年在香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市，其控股股東深業集團是深圳市國資委直管的大
 型綜合性企業集團。公司按照「產城創新建設者、幸福民生運營商」的定位，以物業開發、物業投資及城市綜
 合運營服務為核心業務，同時涉足高科農業、高端製造等創新業務，致力於轉型升級成以城市綜合開發運
 營、科技產業的投資服務為主業的科技型產業集團。

 公司深耕深圳，聚焦粵港澳大灣區，佈局長三角區域及重點省會等高能級城市，目前本集團於國
 內多個城市合共擁有儲備建築面積約660萬平方米的土地儲備，其中位於大灣區的優質土地儲
 備的建築面積約428萬平方米。公司致力優化土地儲備的結構，將資產和業務向大灣區和
 重點區域的高能級城市集中。

 公司一貫之核心策略目標，乃致力推動業務長期持續發展，以期為股東帶來整
 體穩定回報及締造價值增長。

 Chairman’s Statement
 主席報告




 Focusing on the “14th Five-Year Plan” strategy, the Group is committed to              本集團圍繞「十四五」戰略，在促進地產業務穩健發
 transforming and upgrading itself into a technology-based industrial group with        展的同時，積極推動綜合運營業務的拓展，加強創
 a focus on integrated urban development and operation, and investment and              新業務的培育，致力於轉型升級為以城市綜合開發運
 services in the technology industry, while promoting the steady development            營、科技產業的投資服務為主業的科技型產業集團。
 of its property business and actively promoting the expansion of its integrated        面對嚴峻低迷的行業環境，本集團堅持「管理提升、
 operation business and strengthening the cultivation of innovative businesses.         穩中求進」的工作總基調，紮實推進「管理提升年」
 Amid the severe downturn in the industry, the Group adhered to a general               行動計劃，全面落實「疫情要防住、經濟要穩住、發
 keynote of “improving management for prudent growth” and promoted the                  展要安全」的總體要求，攻堅克難，地產業務穩中有
 “year of management improvement” initiative step by step. The Group has fully          進，綜合運營業務蝶變躍升，創新業務加速發展，本
 implemented the overall requirements of “preventing the epidemic, stabilizing          集團在轉型升級和高質量發展的道路上，實現質的有
 the economy, and ensuring safety in development”, overcoming difficulties              效提升，成績來之不易。
 and achieving steady progress in the real estate business. The comprehensive
 operation business has undergone a transformation and achieved rapid
 development, while the innovative business has accelerated its growth. The Group
 has achieved effective qualitative improvement on the road of transformation and
 high-quality development, which is not easy.

 2022 Business Review                                                                   2022年經營情況回顧
 Annual Results                                                                         年度業績
 During the year, the Group realized revenue of HK$31.54 billion, representing a        本年內，本集團實現營業額315.4億港元，較去年下
 decrease of 1.6% over last year. Gross profit was HK$10.69 billion, with overall       降1.6%；毛利106.9億港元，整體毛利率為33.9%。
 gross profit margin of 33.9%. Profit attributable to equity shareholders of the        本公司權益股東應佔溢利20.9億港元，每股基本盈利
 Company was HK$2.09 billion, and basic earnings per share was HK23.43                  為23.43港仙，實現扭虧為盈。如剔除本集團所屬投
 cents. If excluding the net effect of changes in fair value of investment properties   資物業公允價值及金融資產公允價值變動的淨影響，
 attributable to the Group and fair value of financial assets, profit attributable to   本公司權益股東應佔溢利31.0億港元。董事會建議以
 equity shareholders of the Company was HK$3.10 billion. The Board recommends           現金方式派發2022年末期股息每股12.00港仙。
 the payment of a final dividend of HK12.00 cents per share for 2022 in cash.

 Prudent Growth in Real Estate Business                                                 地產主業穩中有升
 Against the backdrop of the recurring COVID-19 and market downturn, the Group          在疫情反覆、市場下行的背景下，本集團地產主業精
 intensified the development of the real estate business as a principal business,       耕細作，發揮了經營業績壓艙石的作用。
 enabling it to work as a ballast of operating results.

 Contracted sales remained positive growth. Notwithstanding a nearly 40% year-          合約銷售保持正增長。在重點100城銷售同比降幅接
 on-year sales decline in 100 key cities, the Group maintained strengthened             近四成的情況下，本集團加強營銷創新和重點項目推
 marketing innovation and key projects promotion. It achieved contracted sales          售，全年合同銷售金額約192.1 億元人民幣（折合約

 Expansion of Land Resources                                                      土地資源拓展
 In 2022, adhering to the strategy of “selecting the best among the best”, the    2022 年，本集團秉持「優中選優」的策略，把握時

Impact of the Pandemic                                                               疫情影響
In 2022, with the continuous optimization of the anti-pandemic measures, China       2022年中國疫情防控措施不斷優化調整，實現了疫情
has entered a new stage of pandemic prevention and control smoothly. The             防控平穩轉段。本集團積極採取各項措施應對疫情，
Group took various measures to fight against the pandemic, assumed social            勇於承擔社會責任，穩妥推進各項經營工作。
responsibility with determination, and steadily pushed forward operations.

In terms of sales, the Group took various measures to boost sales performance        在銷售方面，本集團多措並舉推動銷售，有效降低疫
and effectively reduce the impact of the pandemic on it, realizing stable sales      情對銷售的影響，實現了銷售平穩增長；在工程建設
growth. In terms of project construction, the Group has established a proper         方面，本集團建立妥善的管控機制，在保障安全和質
control mechanism to ensure safety and quality, while accelerating the progress      量的前提下，緊抓工程進度，各項目如期結轉。為應
of construction works, thus all projects have been settled as scheduled. In terms    對疫情防控本集團相應的成本及費用有所增加。在資
of capital, given the increased costs and expenses incurred during the pandemic      金方面，本集團同時加大了集中籌措力度和融資渠道
prevention and control, the Group has stepped up efforts in centralized financing    拓展，疫情的散發沒有對集團的流動性產生影響。
and expanding financing channels, and the pandemic did not have any impact on
the liquidity of the Group.

 Policy Risks                                                                           政策風險
 Throughout 2022, the policy for real estate industry in China was to maintain a        2022年中國地產行業政策面以保持房地產市場平穩發
 stable development of the real estate market, with aim to realize a virtuous circle.   展貫穿全年，定調良性循環。「房住不炒」作為宏觀
 As the keynote of the macropolicy, “house is a place to live instead of a tool for     政策主要基調，將繼續完善住房市場，促進市場進一
 speculation” will continue to improve the housing market and promote the market        步邁向平穩健康發展，配合金融政策的糾偏、因城施
 to further move towards a stable and healthy development. Together with the            策空間的打開以及新的發展模式的探索，房地產市場
 correction of financial policies, the opening of city-orientated policy-making space   將保持平穩發展。本集團將仔細解讀政策並密切關注
 and the exploration of new development patterns, the real estate market will           市場情況，靈活調整公司策略和銷售計劃以應對政策
 maintain stable development. The Group will carefully interpret the policies and       變化帶來的風險。
 closely monitor the market situation, and flexibly adjust the Company’s strategy
 and sales plan to cope with the risk from policy changes.

 Exchange Rate Risks                                                                    匯率風險
 There is a fluctuation in Renminbi exchange rate. The Group’s main assets are          人民幣匯率存在波動風險。本集團主要資產位於中國
 located in mainland China while the reporting currency of Group’s financial            大陸，而本集團財務報告的呈報貨幣為港元。人民幣
 reports is Hong Kong dollar. The fluctuation in Renminbi exchange rate will            匯率波動，將導致本集團以人民幣計值之資產及收益
 cause changes in the value of the Group’s assets and earnings denominated in           相對呈報貨幣港元價值變動。本集團年內未採用金融
 Renminbi against Hong Kong dollar, the reporting currency. The Group did not           工具對沖匯率風險，未來本集團將監控匯率波動，積
 adopt any financial instrument to hedge against exchange rate risk in the year.        極通過多種方式應對匯率風險。
 In future, the Group will monitor the exchange rate fluctuations, and actively
 address exchange rate risks through a variety of ways.

 estate business. Inadequate internal processes, personal misconduct, external          所影響。內部流程不足、員工個人失當行為、外部合

 may have certain negative impacts on the Group’s operation. The Group will             營運構成若干負面影響。本集團將繼續優化管理，健
 continue to optimize its management, improve its internal control system and           全內控體系，提升運營能力以規避相關風險。
 enhance its operational capabilities so as to avoid such risks.

 Corporate Governance Practices                                                          企業管治常規
 The Company is committed to maintaining high standard of corporate governance.          本公司致力恪守奉行高標準之企業管治。董事會（「董
 The board of Directors (the “Board”) strongly believes that good corporate              事會」）堅信，良好企業管治對有效之管理、健全之
 governance is essential for effective management, a healthy corporate culture,          企業文化、成功之業務發展，以及提升股東價值至關
 successful business growth and enhancing shareholders’ value. The Company               重要。本公司已於整個2022年應用並遵守香港聯合交
 has applied the principles and complied with all code provisions of the Corporate       易所有限公司證券上市規則（「上市規則」）附錄十四
 Governance Code (“CG Code”) as set out in Appendix 14 to the Rules Governing            所載之企業管治守則（「企業管治守則」）之所有原則

 Rules”) throughout the year 2022.

The Board                                                                            董事會
Role and Delegation                                                                  角色及授權
Under the leadership of the Chairman, the Board is responsible for shaping and       董事會在主席的領導下，負責塑造及監督企業文化，
overseeing the corporate culture, formulating overall strategies and policies of     制定本公司的整體策略及政策，監督及評估公司的業
the Company, supervising and evaluating business and financial performance of        務與財務表現；釐定適用於公司情況的合適企業管治
the Company, determining appropriate corporate governance practices applicable       常規以及通過建立健全的企業管治架構及制定適當的
to the Company’s circumstances and ensuring operational efficiency and               風險控制措施，確保其營運的效率及效益，致力提升
effectiveness by establishing a comprehensive corporate governance framework         長遠股東價值。
with appropriate risk control measures and aiming to enhance long-term
shareholders’ value.

 Remuneration Committee                                                               薪酬委員會
 The Remuneration Committee comprises three members, all being Independent            薪 酬 委 員 會 由 三 名 成 員（ 均 為 獨 立 非 執 行 董 事 ）組
 Non-executive Directors. It is chaired by Dr. WONG Yau Kar, David and other          成。薪酬委員會由黃友嘉博士擔任主席，其他成員
 members are Mr. LI Wai Keung, Mr. WU Wai Chung, Michael (ceased to be                為李偉強先生、吳偉驄先生（自2022年12月31日起
 a member on 31 December 2022) and Prof. GONG Peng (appointed on 31                   不再為成員）及宮鵬教授（於2022 年12 月31 日獲委
 December 2022).                                                                      任）。

 The Company adopted the model whereby the Remuneration Committee is                  本公司已採納由薪酬委員會負責根據其職權範圍就個
 responsible for making recommendation to the Board on the remuneration               別執行董事及高級管理人員的薪酬待遇向董事會提出
 packages of individual Executive Director and senior management according to its     建議的模式。薪酬委員會亦負責就非執行董事的薪酬
 terms of reference. The Remuneration Committee is also responsible for making        事項向董事會提出建議、設立恰當及透明的程序以制
 recommendation to the Board on the remuneration matters of Non-executive             訂薪酬、根據上市規則第17章審閱及 ╱ 或批准購股權
 Directors, the setting up of adequate and transparent procedures for setting         計劃相關事項。薪酬委員會的職權範圍全文可於本公
 such remuneration, reviewing and/or approving matters relating to share option       司網站及聯交所網站內查閱。
 schemes under Chapter 17 of the Listing Rules. The full terms of reference of the
 Remuneration Committee are available on the Company’s website and the Stock
 Exchange’s website.

 The Group’s Remuneration Policy aims to ensure that the remuneration level is        本集團的薪酬政策旨在確保薪酬水平適當並與本公司
 appropriate and align with the Company’s goals, objectives and performance,          的目標、宗旨及表現保持一致，以便招聘及挽留優秀
 so as to facilitate the recruitment and retention of outstanding directors, senior   董事、高級管理人員及員工，並為彼等提供充足動力
 management and employees and to provide them with adequate incentive                 以達成業務目標。於釐定董事及僱員薪酬時，須考
 for achieving business targets. When determining Directors’ and employees’           慮包括整體市場及房地產行業薪酬水平；相關職位及
 remuneration, factors including salary levels in the market as a whole and in        工作領域的市場基準；本集團及個人的表現在內的因
 the real estate industry; market benchmarks for relevant positions and work          素。亦將根據個人表現及本集團業績向僱員提供花紅
 areas; the performance of the Group and the individual etc. shall be considered.     及其他獎勵（包括購股權）。此外，薪酬方案每年進
 Bonuses and other incentives including share options will also be provided to        行一次審閱，以確保內部公平及競爭力。
 employees based on individual performance and the results of the Group. And,
 remuneration packages are reviewed on an annual basis to ensure internal equity
 and competitiveness.

 No Director or any of his/her associates is allowed to determine their own           任何董事或其任何聯繫人士不得自行釐訂其本身的薪
 remuneration. In making recommendation to the Board on the remuneration of           酬。在向董事會建議董事及高級管理人員的薪酬時，
 Directors and senior management, the Remuneration Committee will take into           薪酬委員會將考慮本集團薪酬政策所載的因素。
 account the factors set out in the Group’s Remuneration Policy.

 management and make recommendation to the Board regarding the remuneration           事調任為非執行董事）以及年內獲委任為董事的王昱
 of Mr. DONG Fang, who was re-designated from Executive Director to Non-              文先生、徐恩利先生及宮鵬教授的薪酬向董事會提供

 who were appointed Directors, during the year. The Remuneration Committee            構，並審議了各種以表現為基準的激勵安排（包括購

Risk Management and Internal Control                                                風險管理與內部監控
Responsibility                                                                      責任
The Board is responsible for the risk management and internal control systems       董事會須對風險管理及內部監控系統負責，有責任檢
and reviewing the effectiveness of such systems. Management is responsible for      討該等制度的有效性，而管理層則負責設計及執行內
designing and implementing an internal control system to manage the risks to the    部監控制度以最大限度的管理公司面臨的風險，達
Company to the maximum extent, in order to identify and manage these risks so       到識別和管理這些風險，從而可以掌握、降低、舒
as to understand, reduce, mitigate, divert or avoid these risks. The Board hereby   緩、轉移或規避這些風險。董事會謹在此闡釋該等系
explains that the systems are designed to manage, rather than eliminate, the risk   統旨在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標的風險，而且
of failure to meet business objectives, and only to provide reasonable, but not     只能就不會有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕
absolute, assurance against material misstatement or loss.                          對的保證。

Risk Management Structure                                                           風險管理架構


 Board Oversight                                                                    董事會監督
 Audit Committee, acting on behalf of the Board                                     代表董事會行事的審核委員會
 •     Ensure that an appropriate and effective risk management framework is        •   確保公司已設立及維持一個合適和有效的風
       established and maintained by the Company.                                       險管理架構。

 •     Oversee management in the design, implementation and monitoring of           •   監督管理層在風險管理架構的設計、執行及
       the risk management framework.                                                   監察方面的工作。

 •     Oversee management in their risk mitigation efforts.                         •   監督管理層在風險紓緩方面的工作。

 Risk Management, Reporting and Communication                                       風險管理、匯報及溝通
 Risk Management Department                                                         風險管理部
 •     Being the specialized risk management department, is responsible for         •   為風險管理專職部門，負責公司全面風險管
       the establishment and operation of the comprehensive risk management             理體系建設與運作的具體工作；指導、督促
       system of the Company; guide and supervise the overall risk                      其他各部門及各所屬企業開展全面風險管理
       management of other departments and the respective enterprises which             工作。
       they belong to.

 •     Risk management: Regularly conduct systematic risk evaluation and            •   風險管理：定期進行系統風險評估、對重大
       carry out risk audit to major businesses in advance.                             業務進行事前風險審核。

 •     Internal control: inspect, evaluate and optimize the implementation of       •   內部控制：檢查、評價和優化內控制度的執
       internal control system, to avoid execution risks.                               行情況，規避執行上的風險。

 Risk Management Process                                                                 風險管理程序
 The Company adopts an integrated “bottom-up” and “top-down” risk review                 公司採用「由下而上」的方式配合「由上而下」的綜合
 process, requiring the heads of functional departments and business units of the        風險檢討程序，要求公司職能部門及業務部門主管參
 Company to participate in identifying operational risks and incorporate them into       與識別營運風險，納入各項業務及決策流程中，包括
 various business and decision-making processes, including strategy formulation,         策略制定、業務發展、業務規劃、資金分配、投資
 business development, business planning, capital allocation, investment                 決定、內部監控及日常營運等，以便於
 decisions, internal controls and daily operations, to enable

 (1)   comprehensive identification and prioritization of all significant risks within   (1)   全面識別公司內部所有的重大風險，並進行優
       the Company;                                                                            次排序；

 (2)   reporting of significant risks to the appropriate management level;               (2)   將重大風險上報至適當的管理層級別；

 (3)   the management to effectively communicate about the risks.                        (3)   讓管理層就風險進行有效溝通。

Bottom-up Process                                                                 由下而上的程序

•   Business units and functional departments of the Company conduct risk         •   業務部門和公司職能部門對職責範圍內的具
    identification and evaluation of specific businesses within the scope of          體業務進行風險辨識、評估，提出和實施風
    responsibility, propose and implement risk management solutions and               險管理解決方案；並年度須向公司風險管理
    report annually to the risk management department of the Company the              部匯報在風險管理過程中識別的重大風險。
    significant risks identified during the risk management process.

    own business processes, collecting and collating internal and external            持續地收集、整理本部門職能範圍內的內
    risk initial information within their scope of functions extensively and          部、外部風險初始信息。風險管理部定期
    continuously. The risk management department regularly screens,                   對各部門收集的風險初始信息進行必要的篩
    refines, compares, classifies and combines the initial risk information           選、提煉、對比、分類、組合，並根據各
    collected by each department as necessary and draws the risk                      部門的風險評估結果，繪製風險坐標圖，初
    coordinates map based on the risk evaluation results of each department           步確定公司本部各項風險的管理優先順序、
    to initially determine the management priorities of various risks of the          制定風險管理策略和提出和實施風險管理解
    Company, formulate risk management strategies, and propose and                    決方案。
    implement risk management solutions.

    screening and sequencing and interview procedures, compiles the                   驟以及訪談程序，編寫企業全面風險管理年
    annual comprehensive risk management work report and the “Evaluation              度工作報告及《內部監控及風險管理系統評
    Report on Internal Control and Risk Management Systems”.                          估報告》。

    Internal Control and Risk Management Systems” to the Audit Committee              報告》供審核委員會及董事會審閱。
    and the Board for their review.

    internal control systems of the Company on an annual basis.                       效。

    functional departments is responsible for identifying and evaluating the          別從投資審核及戰略規劃、財務管理、法律

    risks.

    out systematic significant risk evaluation, identify, evaluate and analyze        性重大風險評估工作，對當前經營發展中存

Internal Auditing                                                                       內部審計
The Risk Management Department is responsible for the internal audit of the             風險管理部門負責公司內部審計，運用其設立的《全
Company. It reviews the risks involved in daily operations of the Company               面風險管理手冊》及《內部審計工作管理暫行辦法》中
and its subsidiaries by using the audit plans and procedures set out in the             清晰列明的審核計劃及程序，檢討公司及附屬企業的
“Comprehensive Risk Management Manual” and the “Interim Measures for                    日常運營面臨的風險，並對內部審計項目做定期後評
Internal Audit Management” it established, and conducts regular post-evaluation         價檢討以保證風險管理架構的成效。
reviews on internal audit items to ensure the effectiveness of risk management
framework.

Each year, the Risk Management Department determines the internal audit work            風險管理部每年度按照公司戰略部署，根據經營管理
priorities and formulates annual internal audit plans according to the Company’s        需要和公司的年度工作安排，確定內部審計工作重
strategic deployment, business management needs and annual work schedule.               點，制定年度內部審計計劃。

The annual internal audit plan shall include the following basic elements:              年度內部審計計劃當包括以下基本內容：

(“HKSAs”) issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities under those standards are          進行審計。我們在該等準則下承擔的責任已在本報告

Key Audit Matters                                                                       關鍵審計事項

and we do not provide a separate opinion on these matters.

•     Valuation of investment properties                                                •   投資物業估值

detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can arise from             發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預
fraud or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they   期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者

      concern basis of accounting and, based on the audit evidence obtained,                論。根據所獲取的審計憑證，確定是否存在與
      whether a material uncertainty exists related to events or conditions that            事項或情況有關的重大不確定性，從而可能導
      may cast significant doubt on the Group’s ability to continue as a going              致對 貴集團的持續經營能力產生重大疑慮。
      concern. If we conclude that a material uncertainty exists, we are required           如果我們認為存在重大不確定性，則有必要在
      to draw attention in our auditor’s report to the related disclosures in the           核數師報告中提請使用者注意綜合財務報表中的
      consolidated financial statements or, if such disclosures are inadequate,             相關披露。假若有關的披露不足，則我們應當
      to modify our opinion. Our conclusions are based on the audit evidence                發表非無保留意見。我們的結論是基於核數師
      obtained up to the date of our auditor’s report. However, future events or            報告日止所取得的審計憑證。然而，未來事項
      conditions may cause the Group to cease to continue as a going concern.               或情況可能導致 貴集團不能持續經營。

2.2 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                                  2.2 合併及權益會計原則
    (a) Subsidiaries                                                                       (a) 附屬公司
         Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over which              附屬公司均為本集團擁有控制權的所有實
         the Group has control. The Group controls an entity where the Group                   體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團承擔或
         is exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from its involvement                擁有自其參與該實體產生之可變回報之
         with the entity and has the ability to affect those returns through                   權利及有能力透過行使權力以影響其回報
         its power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are fully              時，本集團即控制該實體。附屬公司自控
         consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to the                     制權轉移至本集團之日起全面合併。附屬
         Group. They are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                     公司自控制停止之日起解除合併。

           The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for business                     本集團使用收購會計法對業務合併進行會
           combinations by the group (refer to note 2.3).                                           計處理（參閱附註2.3）。

           Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains on                             公司之間之交易、集團公司間交易之結餘
           transactions between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised                          及未變現收益予以對銷。未變現虧損亦予
           losses are also eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence                      以對銷，除非交易有證據證明已轉讓資產
           of an impairment of the transferred asset. Accounting policies                           出現減值。附屬公司會計政策已按需要作
           of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                              出變動，以確保與本集團所採納之政策貫
           consistency with the policies adopted by the group.                                      徹一致。

           Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries                      附屬公司業績及權益中的非控股權益分別
           are shown separately in the consolidated statement of profit or loss,                    於綜合損益表、全面收益表、權益變動表
           statement of comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity                        及財務狀況表中單獨呈列。
           and statement of financial position respectively.

 2     Summary of significant accounting policies (continued)                            2   主要會計政策概要（續）
 2.2 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting (continued)                       2.2 合併及權益會計原則（續）
     (b) Associates                                                                          (b) 聯營公司
          Associates are all entities over which the group has significant                       聯營公司指所有本集團對其擁有重大影響
          influence but not control or joint control. This is generally the case                 力但無控制權或聯合控制權的實體。一般
          where the group holds between 20% and 50% of the voting rights.                        情況下，本集團持有20%至50%投票權。
          Investments in associates are accounted for using the equity method                    於聯營公司之投資於初步按成本確認後採
          of accounting, after initially being recognised at cost.                               用權益會計法入賬。

       (c)   Joint arrangements                                                              (c)   合營安排
             Under HKFRS 11 Joint Arrangements investments in joint                                根據香港財務報告準則第11號聯合安排，
             arrangements are classified as either joint operations or joint                       於聯合安排的投資被分類為聯合經營或合
             ventures. The classification depends on the contractual rights and                    營公司。該分類取決於各投資者的合約性
             obligations of each investor, rather than the legal structure of the                  權利及責任，而非聯合安排的法律架構。

 2.3 Business combinations                                                                 2.3 業務合併
     The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for all business                  本集團採用收購會計法就所有業務合併入賬，
     combinations, regardless of whether equity instruments or other assets are                而無論有否收購股權投資或其他資產。收購一
     acquired. The consideration transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary               間附屬公司的轉讓代價包括：
     comprises the:

       •      fair values of the assets transferred                                            •   為所轉讓資產公允值

       •      liabilities incurred to the former owners of the acquired business               •   對被收購業務前擁有人所產生的負債

       •      equity interests issued by the group                                             •   本集團所發行股權

       •      fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a contingent                 •   或有代價安排產生的任何資產或負債的公
              consideration arrangement, and                                                       允值，及

       •      fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the subsidiary.                •   附屬公司任何先前存在股權的公允值。

       Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent liabilities assumed         於業務合併時所收購的可識別資產及所承擔的
       in a business combination are, with limited exceptions, measured initially              負債及或有負債，初始按收購當日的公允值計
       at their fair values at the acquisition date. The group recognises any non-             量，惟少數例外情況除外。本集團根據個別收
       controlling interest in the acquired entity on an acquisition-by-acquisition            購交易按公允值或非控股權益應佔被收購實體可
       basis either at fair value or at the non-controlling interest’s proportionate           識別資產淨值的比例確認於被收購實體的任何非
       share of the acquired entity’s net identifiable assets.                                 控股權益。

       Concentration test as prescribed by HKFRS 3 “Business Combination”                      本集團已應用香港財務報告準則第3號「業務合
       has been applied by the Group to consider whether the set of activities                 併」所規定的集中度測試，以考慮所收購附屬公
       and assets of subsidiaries acquired are individually businesses. If the fair            司的一系列活動及資產是否為個別業務。倘所
       value of total net assets acquired substantially equals to the fair value of            收購資產淨值總額的公允值與已收購可識別資產
       an identifiable asset acquired, the transaction would be accounted for as               的公允值大致相若，則交易將作為資產收購而
       asset acquisition instead of business acquisition. The consideration is then            非業務收購入賬。屆時會將代價直接分配至收
       directly allocated to the individual identifiable assets acquired and liabilities       購的個別可識別資產及承擔的負債。
       assumed.

       Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                                     收購相關成本於產生時支銷。

                    position presented are translated at the closing rate at the date                   該財務狀況表日的收市匯率換算為呈
                    of that statement of financial position;                                            報貨幣；

                    exchange rates (unless this is not a reasonable approximation of                    各個交易日期通行匯率累計影響的合
                    the cumulative effect of the rates prevailing on the transaction                    理近似值，在該情況下收入及支出
                    dates, in which case income and expenses are translated at the                      於各交易日期率換算）；及
                    dates of the transactions), and

              •     all resulting exchange differences are recognised in other                      •   所有產生的匯兌差額均於其他全面收
                    comprehensive income.                                                               益確認。

              On consolidation, exchange differences arising from the translation                   於綜合賬目時，因換算任何於海外實體的
              of any net investment in foreign entities, and of borrowings and other                投資淨額以及換算被指定為對沖有關投資

              or loss, as part of the gain or loss on sale.                                         收益或虧損的一部份。

2.7 Property, plant and equipment                                                         2.7 物業、廠房及設備
    Property, plant and equipment are stated at historical cost less depreciation             物業、廠房及設備均以歷史成本扣除折舊及任
    and any impairment losses. Historical cost includes expenditure that is                   何減值虧損列賬。歷史成本包括購買該等項目
    directly attributable to the acquisition of the items.                                    直接應佔的開支。

     Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s carrying amount or recognised                 後續成本只有在很可能為本集團帶來與該項目有
     as a separate asset, as appropriate, only when it is probable that future                  關的未來經濟利益，而該項目的成本能可靠計
     economic benefits associated with the item will flow to the Group and                      量時，才包括在資產的賬面值或確認為一項單
     the cost of the item can be measured reliably. The carrying amount of                      獨資產（如適用）。已更換部份的賬面值已被終
     the replaced part is derecognised. All other repairs and maintenance are                   止確認。所有其他維修及保養開支乃於其產生
     charged to profit or loss during the financial period in which they are                    之財政期間內自損益扣除。
     incurred.

 2.10 Leases (continued)                                                              2.10 租賃（續）
      Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension options are             根據合理確定延期選擇權支付之租賃付款亦計入
      also included in the measurement of the liability.                                   負債計量內。

       The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in the          租賃付款採用租賃所隱含之利率予以貼現。倘
       lease. If that rate cannot be readily determined, the lessee’s incremental         無法輕易釐定該利率，則使用承租人的增量借
       borrowing rate is used, being the rate that the individual lessee would have       款利率，即個別承租人在類似條款及條件的類
       to pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of similar value in        似經濟環境中借入獲得類似價值資產所需資金所
       a similar economic environment with similar terms and conditions.                  必須支付的利率。

       To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:                            為釐定增量借款利率，本集團：

       •      where possible, uses recent third-party financing received by the           •   在可能情況下，使用個別承租人最近獲得
              individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to reflect changes in           的第三方融資為出發點作出調整，以反映
              financing conditions since third party financing was received;                  自獲得第三方融資以來融資狀況之變動；

       •      uses a build-up approach that starts with a risk-free interest rate         •   使用累加法，首先就本集團所持有租賃
              adjusted for credit risk for leases held by the Group, which does not           （最近並無第三方融資者）的信貸風險調整
              have recent third party financing; and                                          無風險利率；及

       •      makes adjustments specific to the lease, e.g. term, country, currency       •   對租賃作出特定調整，例如期限、國家、
              and security.                                                                   貨幣及抵押。

       If a readily observable amortising loan rate is available to the individual        倘個別承租人可獲得與租賃付款情況類似且隨時
       lessee (through recent financing or market data) which has a similar               可觀察之攤銷貸款利率（透過近期融資或市場數
       payment profile to the lease, then the Group entities use that rate as a           據），則集團內實體將該利率作為釐定增量借款
       starting point to determine the incremental borrowing rate.                        利率之出發點。

       Lease payments are allocated between principal and finance cost. The               租賃付款於本金及財務成本之間分配。財務成
       finance cost is charged to profit or loss over the lease period so as to           本於租賃期內自損益中扣除，以便各期間的負
       produce a constant periodic rate of interest on the remaining balance of the       債餘額產生持續的定期利率。
       liability for each period.

       Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the following:                 使用權資產按成本計量，包括以下各項：

       •      the amount of the initial measurement of lease liabilities;                 •   租賃負債的初始計量金額；

       •      any lease payments made at or before the commencement date less             •   於開始日期或之前所作的任何租賃付款，
              any lease incentives received;                                                  減去所得的任何租賃獎勵；

       •      any initial direct costs; and                                               •   任何初始直接成本；及

       •      restoration costs.                                                          •   修復成本。

2.13 Impairment of financial assets                                                       2.13 金融資產減值
     The Group recognises a loss allowance for expected credit losses (“ECL”) on               本集團就根據香港財務報告準則第9號須予減值
     financial assets which are subject to impairment under HKFRS 9 (including                 的金融資產（包括貿易及其他應收款、給予合營
     trade and other receivables, loans to joint ventures and associates, cash                 公司及聯營公司之貸款、現金及現金等價物、
     and cash equivalents, restricted cash and bank deposits and financial                     受限制現金及銀行存款以及財務擔保）的預期信
     guarantee).                                                                               用損失（「預期信用損失」）確認損失撥備。

      The amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date to reflect changes in                 預期信用損失金額於各報告日更新，反映自初
      credit risk since initial recognition. Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that               始確認起的信用風險變動。存續期預期信用損
      will result from all possible default events over the expected life of the                失指相關工具的預期使用期內所有可能的違約事
      relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”) represents the                 件產生之預期信用損失。相反，12個月預期信
      portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from default events that               用損失（「12 個月預期信用損失」）將預期於報
      are possible within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessment are                    告日期後12 個月內可能發生的違約事件預期導
      done based on the Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted for                 致之存續期預期信用損失部份。評估乃根據本
      factors that are specific to the debtors, general economic conditions and an              集團的歷史信用損失經驗進行，並根據債務人
      assessment of both the current conditions at the reporting date as well as                特有的因素、一般經濟狀況以及對報告日期當
      the forecast of future conditions.                                                        前狀況的評估以及對未來狀況的預測作出調整。

      The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade receivables. To                        本集團始終就應收賬款確認整個存續期預期信用
      measure ECL, trade receivables have been grouped based on shared credit                   損失。為計量預期信用損失，應收賬款已按共
      risk characteristics and the days past due. The ECL on these assets are                   有之信貸風險特徵及逾期天數分組。該等資產
      assessed using simplified expected credit loss model.                                     之預期信用損失乃使用簡化預期信用損失模式評
                                                                                                估。

      For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss allowance equal to                 對於所有其他工具，本集團計量的損失撥備相
      12m ECL, unless when there has been a significant increase in credit risk                 當於12個月預期信用損失，除非自初始確認起
      since initial recognition, the Group recognizes lifetime ECL. The assessment              信用風險出現顯著增長，則本集團確認存續期
      of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised is based on significant                      間預期信用損失。是否應確認存續期預期信用
      increases in the likelihood or risk of a default occurring since initial                  損失的評估乃基於自初始確認以來發生違約之可
      recognition.                                                                              能性或風險的顯著增加。

2.14 Offsetting financial instruments                                                     2.14 抵銷金融工具
     Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount reported in                若具法律強制執行權利以抵銷已確認款項及有
     the consolidated statement of financial position when there is a legally                  意按淨額基準結算，或同時變現資產及清償負
     enforceable right to offset the recognised amounts and there is an                        債，則金融資產及負債可予以抵銷，而有關淨
     intention to settle on a net basis or realise the asset and settle the liability          額則於綜合財務狀況表內呈報。法定可執行權
     simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not be contingent on                   利必須不得依賴未來事件而定，而在一般業務
     future events and must be enforceable in the normal course of business                    過程中以及倘本公司或對手方一旦出現違約、
     and in the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the company or the               無償債能力或破產時，這也必須具有約束力。
     counterparty.

 2.15 Properties under development                                                      2.15 發展中物業
      Properties under development are stated at the lower of cost and net                   發展中物業按成本及可變現淨值兩者之較低者列
      realisable value. Net realisable value is determined by reference to the               賬。可變現淨值乃參考於日常業務中已售出物
      sale proceeds of properties sold in the ordinary course of business, less              業的銷售所得款項減去適用的銷售開支及預計完
      applicable selling expenses and the anticipated costs to completion, or by             工成本釐定，或者根據現行市況經由管理層估
      management estimates based on prevailing marketing conditions.                         計得出。

       Development cost of property comprises cost of land use rights,                      物業開發成本包括土地使用權成本、建造成
       construction costs, depreciation of machinery and equipment, borrowing               本、機器及設備折舊、資本化的合資格資產
       costs capitalised for qualifying assets and professional fees incurred during        借貸成本及開發期間招致的專業費用。於完工
       the development period. On completion, the properties are transferred to             後，相關物業轉撥至持作待售之已落成物業。
       completed properties held for sale.

       Properties under development are classified as current assets when the               除非預期有關物業發展項目的建築期長於一般營
       construction of the relevant properties commences unless the construction            運週期，否則發展中物業於有關物業的建築期
       period of the relevant property development project is expected to complete          間開始時分類為流動資產。一般營運週期通常
       beyond normal operating cycle. The normal operating cycle is usually                 為3年左右。
       around 3 years.

       Costs to fulfill a contract comprise the development cost and land use               履行合約的成本包括與現有合約直接相關的開發
       right cost directly related to an existing contract that will be used to             成本及土地使用權成本，將用於履行未來的履
       satisfy performance obligations in the future. The costs to fulfill a contract       約義務。如履行合約的成本預計可收回，則入
       are recorded in properties under development if they are expected to be              賬列為發展中物業。履行合約的成本基於系統
       recovered. The amount is amortised on a systematic basis, consistent                 的方法進行攤銷，與該資產相關的合約收入確
       with the pattern of revenue recognition of the contract to which the asset           認模式一致。
       relates.

 2.16 Completed properties held for sale                                                2.16 持作待售之已落成物業

      of cost and net realisable value.                                                      值兩者之較低者列賬。

       Cost comprises development costs attributable to the unsold properties.              成本包括未出售物業應佔的開發成本。

       conditions.

2.18 Trade and other receivables                                                        2.18 貿易及其他應收款
     Trade receivables are amounts due from buyers for properties sold or                    應收賬款乃於日常業務過程中就銷售物業或提供

     and other receivables is expected in one year or less (or in the normal                 預期於一年或更短時間內（或於正常業務營運週
     operating cycle of the business if longer), they are classified as current              期（如較長））收回，則會被分類為流動資產。
     assets. If not, they are presented as non-current assets.                               否則，在非流動資產中列報。

     Trade and other receivables are recognised initially at fair value and                   應收賬款及其他應收賬款初始按公允值確認，

     method, less provision for impairment.                                                   撥備計量。

2.19 Contract assets and contract liabilities                                           2.19 合約資產及合約負債
     Upon entering into a contract with a buyer, the Group obtains rights to                 在與買家簽訂合約時，本集團有權獲得來自買
     receive consideration from the buyer and assumes performance obligations                家支付的合約價款，與此同時承擔將貨物或服
     to transfer goods or provide services to the buyer. The combination of                  務轉移給買家的履約義務。這些權利和履行義
     those rights and performance obligations gives rise to a net asset or a                 務的結合導致了淨資產或淨負債的產生，這取
     net liability depending on the relationship between the remaining rights                決於剩餘權利和履行義務之間的關係。合約計
     and the performance obligations. The contract is an asset and recognised                量的剩餘收款權超過了剩餘履約義務的部份確認
     as contract assets if the measure of the remaining rights exceeds the                   為合約資產。反之，剩餘履約義務超過剩餘收
     measure of the remaining performance obligations. Conversely, the contract              款權的部份確認為合約負債。
     is a liability and recognised as contract liabilities if the measure of the
     remaining performance obligations exceeds the measure of the remaining
     rights.

     The Group recognises the incremental costs of obtaining a contract with                  本集團為獲得與買家簽訂的合約而產生的增量成
     a buyer as contract acquisition cost within contract assets if the Group                 本預計可收回時，則將其確認為合約資產中的
     expects to recover those costs.                                                          合約取得成本。

     institutions, other short-term, highly liquid investments with original                 其他短期高流通投資（原本到期日不超過三個

     in value, and bank overdrafts. Bank overdrafts are shown within borrowings              財務狀況表中，銀行透支列入流動負債的借款

 2.26 Current and deferred income tax                                                     2.26 當期及遞延所得稅
      The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax payable on the                期內所得稅費用或抵免為當期應課稅收入按各司
      current period’s taxable income based on the applicable income tax rate for              法權區適用所得稅率之應付稅項，經暫時差異
      each jurisdiction adjusted by changes in deferred tax assets and liabilities             及未使用稅項虧損產生的遞延稅項資產及負債變
      attributable to temporary differences and to unused tax losses.                          動調整。

       (a)   Current income tax                                                               (a)   即期所得稅
             The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of the tax                    即期所得稅開支乃根據本公司及其附屬公
             laws enacted or substantively enacted at the end of the reporting                      司和聯營公司業務所在並產生應課稅收入
             period in the countries where the company and its subsidiaries                         之國家於各報告期末已頒佈或實質已頒佈
             and associates operate and generate taxable income. Management                         之稅法計算。管理層就適用稅務法規詮釋
             periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect to                  所規限的情況定期評估報稅表的狀況，並
             situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation             權衡稅務機關是否可能接受不確定稅務處
             and considers whether it is probable that a taxation authority will                    理。本集團基於最大可能金額或預期價值
             accept an uncertain tax treatment. The Group measures its tax                          計量稅項結餘，取決於哪種方法提供不確
             balances either based on the most likely amount or the expected                        定性解決方法的更佳預測。
             value, depending on which method provides a better prediction of the
             resolution of the uncertainty.

       (b)   Deferred income tax                                                              (b)   遞延所得稅
             Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the liability method, on                就資產和負債的稅基與資產和負債在綜合
             temporary differences arising between the tax bases of assets and                      財務報表的賬面值的差額而產生的暫時性
             liabilities and their carrying amounts in the consolidated financial                   差異利用負債法撥備遞延所得稅。然而，
             statements. However, deferred tax liabilities are not recognised if they               倘遞延稅項負債來自對商譽的初始確認，
             arise from the initial recognition of goodwill. Deferred income tax is                 則不作記賬。倘遞延所得稅來自交易（不
             also not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset               包括業務合併）中對資產或負債的初始確
             or liability in a transaction other than a business combination that                   認，而該等資產或負債在交易時不影響會
             at the time of the transaction affects neither accounting nor taxable                  計或應課稅損益及不會導致等同應課稅及
             profit or loss and does not give rise to equal taxable and deductible                  可扣減差異，則同樣不作記賬。遞延所得
             temporary differences. Deferred income tax is determined using tax                     稅以報告期末前頒佈或實質上頒佈的稅率
             rates (and laws) that have been enacted or substantively enacted by                    （及法例）釐定，且該等稅率預期將於相
             the end of the reporting period and are expected to apply when the                     關遞延所得稅資產變現或遞延所得稅負債
             related deferred income tax asset is realised or the deferred income                   償付時應用。
             tax liability is settled.

 2.29 Provisions                                                                         2.29 撥備
      Provisions are recognised when: the Group has a present legal or                        於下列情況下確認撥備：倘本集團因過往事件

      outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation; and the                 流出資源以履行該等責任，而該數額能可靠估
      amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are not recognised for                   計時。未來經營損失撥備不作確認。
      future operating losses.

     obligations may be small.

     to be required to settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate that reflects               特定風險之評估之稅前比率，按照預期履行有
     current market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                      關責任所需支出之現值計量。時間流逝引起之
     specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision due to passage of              撥備增加確認為利息開支。
     time is recognised as interest expense.

2.30 Revenue recognition                                                                2.30 收益確認
     Income is classified by the Group as revenue when it arises from the sale of            當本集團於日常業務過程中提供服務或他人根據
     goods, the provision of services or the use by others of the Group’s assets             租賃使用本集團資產而產生銷售貨品收入時，
     under leases in the ordinary course of the Group’s business.                            本集團將收入分類為收入。

     Revenue is recognised when control over a product or service is transferred              當產品或服務之控制權轉移至客戶或承租人有
     to the customer, or the lessee has the right to use the asset, at the amount             權使用資產時，按本集團預期將享有的承諾代
     of promised consideration to which the Group is expected to be entitled,                 價金額確認收入，惟不包括代第三方收取的款
     excluding those amounts collected on behalf of third parties. Revenue                    項。收入不包括增值稅或其他銷售稅，並經扣
     excludes value added tax or other sales taxes and is after deduction of any              減任何貿易折扣後計算。
     trade discounts.

     Where the contract contains a financing component which provides a                       倘合約載有向客戶提供超過12 個月的重大融資
     significant financing benefit to the customer for more than 12 months,                   利益的融資組成部份，則收入按應收金額的現
     revenue is measured at the present value of the amount receivable,                       值計量，並使用反映於與有關客戶的個別融資
     discounted using the discount rate that would be reflected in a separate                 交易的折現率貼現，且利息收入根據實際利率
     financing transaction with the customer, and interest income is accrued                  法分開累計。倘合約載有向本集團提供重大融
     separately under the effective interest method. Where the contract contains              資利益的融資組成部份，則根據該合約確認的
     a financing component which provides a significant financing benefit to                  收入包括根據實際利率法合約責任附有的利息開
     the Group, revenue recognised under that contract includes the interest                  支。
     expense accreted on the contract liability under the effective interest
     method.

     The Group applies advantage of the practical expedient in paragraph 63                   本集團利用香港財務報告準則第15號第63段中
     of HKFRS 15 and does not adjust the consideration for any effects of a                   的實務簡便做法，並無就融資期為12個月或以
     significant financing component if the period of financing is 12 months or               下的重大融資組成部份的任何影響調整對價。
     less.

2.31 Interest income                                                                     2.31 利息收入
     Interest income is calculated by applying the effective interest rate to the             金融資產之利息收入按實際利率法乘以金融資產
     gross carrying amount of a financial asset except for financial assets that              賬面總額計算，惟後續會發生信用減值之金融
     subsequently become credit-impaired. For credit-impaired financial assets                資產除外。就信貸減值的金融資產而言，其利
     the effective interest rate is applied to the net carrying amount of the                 息收入是用實際利率乘以金融資產賬面淨額（經
     financial asset (after deduction of the loss allowance).                                 扣除損失撥備）得出。

 2.34 Financial guarantee contracts                                                       2.34 財務擔保合約
      Financial guarantee contracts are recognised in respect of the financial                 財務擔保合約乃就本集團為物業購買者向銀行提
      guarantee provided by the Group to the banks for property purchasers.                    供財務擔保確認。

       Financial guarantee liabilities are recognised initially at fair value plus            財務擔保負債初始按公允值加財務擔保負債直接
       transaction costs that are directly attributable to the issue of the financial         產生之交易成本確認。初始確認後，有關負債
       guarantee liabilities. After initial recognition, such liabilities are measured        按履行現行責任所需開支之最佳估計現值與初始
       at the higher of the present value of the best estimate of the expenditure             確認金額減已確認費用累計攤銷之較高者計量。
       required to settle the present obligation and the amount initially recognised
       less cumulative amortisation of fees recognised.

       Financial guarantee liabilities are derecognised from the consolidated                 當且僅當合約中規定之責任解除、取消或屆滿
       statement of financial position when, and only when, the obligation                    時，財務擔保負債自綜合財務狀況表中終止確
       specified in the contract is discharged or cancelled or expired.                       認。

       The Group’s major operations are mainly located in the PRC and is                      本集團之主要業務大部份位於中國，受特定因

       The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks: market risk          本集團因其業務活動面臨各種財務風險：市場
       (mainly including currency risk, interest rate risk and price risk), credit risk       風險（主要包括貨幣風險、利率風險及價格風
       and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk management programme focuses              險）、信貸風險及流動性風險。本集團之整體
       on the unpredictability of financial markets and seeks to minimise potential           風險管理計劃針對金融市場不可預測之特性，

       (c)   Liquidity risk                                                                  (c)   流動性風險
             Liquidity risk arises when the Group is unable to meet its current                    當本集團未能償還其已到期流動負債時則
             liabilities that fall due. The Group’s objective is to maintain a balance             產生流動性風險。本集團旨在透過運用短
             between continuity of funding and flexibility through the use of short                期及長期銀行貸款及其他借貸，維持資金
             and long term bank loans and other borrowings. Through maintaining                    持續供應與靈活性之平衡。透過將其資產
             a reasonable proportion in its asset and liability structure, the Group               負債結構保持在合理比例，本集團可滿足
             is able to meet its ongoing financial needs.                                          其持續財務需要。

 3.2 Capital management                                                                              3.2 資本管理
     The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the Group’s                           本集團管理資本目標為維持本集團持續經營能
     ability to continue as a going concern in order to provide returns for                              力，以為股東提供回報為其他利益相關方創造
     shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal                         利益，並保持最佳資本結構以減低資金成本。
     capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.

       In order to optimise the capital structure, the Group may adjust the amount                           為優化資本架構，本集團可能會調整已向股東
       of dividends paid to shareholders, repurchase of shares, issue new shares                             支付的股息金額、回購股份、發行新股或出售
       or sell assets.                                                                                       資產。

     Estimates and judgements used in preparing the consolidated financial                  評估在編製綜合財務報表時使用的估計及判斷乃
     statements are evaluated and based on historical experience and other                  以過往經驗及其他因素（包括在當時情況下被視

     reasonable under the circumstances. The Group makes estimates and                      來作出估計及假設。根據定義，所得的會計估
     assumptions concerning the future. The resulting accounting estimates will,            計極少與相關的實際結果相同。下文討論可能
     by definition, seldom equal the related actual results. The estimates and              對下個財政年度的資產和負債賬面值有重大影響
     assumptions that may have a significant effect on the carrying amounts of              的估計和假設。

       The Group manages its businesses by business units, which are organised              本集團按業務單位管理業務，而業務單位按其

       and performance assessment, the Group has presented the following five
       reportable segments:

       (a)    the property development segment engages in the development of                (a)   物業開發分類從事發展樓宇；

 16 Investment properties (continued)                                                  16 投資物業（續）
 (a) Fair value hierarchy (continued)                                                  (a) 公允值等級架構（續）
     The fair value of the Group’s investment properties in the Mainland China is          本集團位於中國大陸之投資物業公允值採用收入
     determined using income capitalisation method and residual method.                    資本化法及剩餘法予以釐定。

       Income capitalisation method determines fair values by discounting the            收入資本化法透過採用資本化率折現有關物業之
       projected cash flows associated with the properties using capitalisation          預測現金流量釐定公允值。有關估值考慮到各
       rates. The valuation takes into account expected market rental growth and         物業之預期市場租金增長及空置率。所用貼現
       vacancy rate of the respective properties. The discount rates used have           率乃就樓宇的質素及地點以及租戶信貸質素作
       been adjusted for the quality and location of the buildings and the tenant        出調整。公允值計量與預期市場租金增長正相
       credit quality. The fair value measurement is positively correlated to the        關，惟與空置率及資本化率負相關。
       expected market rental growth, and negatively correlated to the vacancy
       rate and the capitalisation rate.

       The residual method takes into account the residual value of the gross            剩餘法考慮總發展價值並扣除尚未發生的建築成
       development value (“GDV”) after deduction of the outstanding construction         本、開支以及發展利潤後的剩餘價值。剩餘法
       costs and expenses as well as profit element. It involves firstly the             首先計算假設建議發展項目於估值日已落成的合
       assessment of the aggregate GDV of the proposed development, as if                計總發展價值。建議發展項目的估計成本包括
       completed, as at the valuation date. Estimated costs of the proposed              建築成本、市場推廣支出、專業費用、財務費
       development include construction costs, marketing expense, professional           用以及相關成本，另加開發商的風險及利潤撥
       fee, finance charges and associated costs, plus an allowance for the              備。尚未發生的開發成本將由總發展價值中扣
       developer's risk and profit. The outstanding development costs are then           除，並得出標的物業的剩餘價值。
       deducted from the GDV and the resultant figure is the residual value of the
       subject property.

 (b) Undiscounted lease payments under non-cancellable operating                       (b) 不可取消經營租賃之未貼現租賃付款
     leases
     The Group leases out investment properties under operating leases. The              本集團根據經營租賃出租投資物業。租約一般
     leases typically run for an initial period of 1 to 15 years, with an option to      初步為期1 至15 年，並有權於到期日續租，同
     renew the lease after that date at which time all terms are renegotiated.           時重新商討所有條款。租賃付款一般每年增加
     Lease payments are usually increased every year to reflect market rentals.          一次，以反映市場租金。若干租約包含基於租
     Certain leases include variable lease payment terms that are based on the           戶收益的可變租賃付款條款。
     revenue of tenants.

       Undiscounted lease payments under non-cancellable operating leases in             本集團於未來期間應收於報告日期已訂立之不可

30 Share option scheme                                                               30 購股權計劃
   The Company operated a share option scheme (the “Scheme”) which was                     本公司設有一項購股權計劃（「計劃」）於2012
   approved and adopted on 22 June 2012 for the purpose of providing                       年6 月22 日批准及採納，以對本集團業務成
   incentives and rewards to directors and employees of the Group who                      功作出貢獻之本集團董事及僱員提供鼓勵及獎
   contribute to the success of the Group’s operations. The Scheme became                  勵。計劃於2012年6月22日生效，而除非被取
   effective on 22 June 2012 and, unless otherwise cancelled or amended,                   消或經修訂，否則該計劃將於當日起計十年內
   shall be valid and effective for a period of 10 year from that date, after              有效。有效期屆滿後，則不會再授出任何購股
   which period no further options will be issued but in all other respects the            權，但在所有其他方面，計劃條文仍具十足效
   provisions of the Scheme shall remain in full force and effect.                         力及作用。
